`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions.

# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies them into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized |
| Application code | `projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

## developer

<multi_agent_mode>Any earlier instruction enabling proactive multi-agent delegation no longer applies. Do not spawn sub-agents unless the user or applicable AGENTS.md/skill instructions explicitly ask for sub-agents, delegation, or parallel agent work.</multi_agent_mode>

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 8.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c3b273","wall_time_seconds":4.0689963,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3613,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\\r\\n * full sections.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\\r\\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\\r\\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\\r\\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\\r\\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\\r\\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\\r\\n * never collide:\\r\\n *\\r\\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug \\"mc-practice-1\\"\\r\\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug \\"guided::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug \\"drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>\\"\\r\\n *     homework       →  pool_slug \\"drill::assignment::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     everything else→  pool_slug \\"drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>\\"\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\\r\\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  loadPracticeSession,\\r\\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  completePracticeSession,\\r\\n  migratePracticeSession,\\r\\n  practiceSessionIdFor,\\r\\n  PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\\r\\nimport { PracticeSession, TABLES } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { isActivePracticeTestDevice } from \'@/lib/practiceTestStore\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\\r\\nconst SECTION_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::`;\\r\\nconst TEST_LEG_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}practice-test::`;\\r\\nconst accountSectionKey = (key: string) =>\\r\\n  key.startsWith(SECTION_PREFIX) || key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX);\\r\\nconst accountWriteChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction queueAccountWrite(userId: string, key: string, write: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const identity = `${userId}::${key}`;\\r\\n  const previous = accountWriteChains.get(identity) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const current = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(write);\\r\\n  accountWriteChains.set(identity, current);\\r\\n  void current.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (accountWriteChains.get(identity) === current) accountWriteChains.delete(identity);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return current;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertActiveTestLeg(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX)) return;\\r\\n  const runId = key.slice(TEST_LEG_PREFIX.length).split(\'::\')[0];\\r\\n  if (!await isActivePracticeTestDevice(userId, runId)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'This practice test is active on another device.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function readSectionFromAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n    .select(\'data\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'table_name\', TABLES.practice_sessions)\\r\\n    .eq(\'record_id\', practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key))\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(data?.data);\\r\\n  return parsed.success ? migratePracticeSession(parsed.data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function syncSectionToAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const record = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\\r\\n  if (!record) throw new Error(\'The section could not be saved.\');\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\').upsert({\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    table_name: TABLES.practice_sessions,\\r\\n    record_id: record.id,\\r\\n    data: record,\\r\\n  }, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst sectionBackupKey = (userId: string, key: string) =>\\r\\n  `lsatu:section-backup:${practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key)}`;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Synchronous device copy, including the latest unacknowledged choice. */\\r\\nexport function writeSectionDeviceBackup(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\\r\\n): string | null {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key || !accountSectionKey(key) || !snapshot.questionQueue.length) return null;\\r\\n  const previous = readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n  const time = new Date(Math.max(Date.now(), previous ? Date.parse(previous.updated_at) + 1 : 0)).toISOString();\\r\\n  const record = PracticeSession.safeParse({\\r\\n    id: practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key),\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    pool_slug: key,\\r\\n    completion_id: key,\\r\\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n    question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,\\r\\n    current_index: Math.min(Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0), snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1),\\r\\n    attempts: snapshot.attempts,\\r\\n    status: \'in_progress\',\\r\\n    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs,\\r\\n    timer_mode: snapshot.timerMode,\\r\\n    timer_custom_minutes: snapshot.timerCustomMinutes,\\r\\n    regulation_answered_qids: snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids,\\r\\n    started_at: time,\\r\\n    updated_at: time,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (!record.success) return null;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    window.localStorage.setItem(sectionBackupKey(userId, key), JSON.stringify(record.data));\\r\\n    return time;\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\'[drill:section] device backup unavailable\', error);\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction readSectionDeviceBackup(userId: string, key: string): DrillResumeSession | null {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(sectionBackupKey(userId, key));\\r\\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null);\\r\\n    if (!parsed.success || parsed.data.user_id !== userId || parsed.data.pool_slug !== key) return null;\\r\\n    return parsed.data;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction clearSectionDeviceBackup(userId: string, key: string): void {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    window.localStorage.removeItem(sectionBackupKey(userId, key));\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    // The completed account row is authoritative even if browser storage is unavailable.\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeIdentity = {\\r\\n  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\\r\\n  day: number;\\r\\n  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\\r\\nfunction fingerprint(input: string): string {\\r\\n  let h = 2166136261;\\r\\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\\r\\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\\r\\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Key one run\'s resume row.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A homework assignment is identified by the assignment: two assignments of\\r\\n * the same section are two sittings, and a row named by the section alone let\\r\\n * the second one open on the first one\'s answers and the first one\'s spent\\r\\n * clock. Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\\r\\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\\r\\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. Returns null for\\r\\n * the surfaces that own their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for\\r\\n * anything launched from an explicit list of qids, where the caller already\\r\\n * knows the set.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function drillResumeKey(input: {\\r\\n  mode: string;\\r\\n  config?: unknown;\\r\\n  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\\r\\n  /** The classroom homework assignment this run belongs to, when it does. */\\r\\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Full exam legs must never share the standalone section\'s resume row. */\\r\\n  ptRun?: { runId: string; legIndex: number } | null;\\r\\n}): string | null {\\r\\n  if (input.ptRun) {\\r\\n    return `${TEST_LEG_PREFIX}${input.ptRun.runId}::leg-${input.ptRun.legIndex}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (input.assignmentId) {\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}assignment::${input.assignmentId}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const mode = input.mode;\\r\\n  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\\r\\n    if (!config) return null;\\r\\n    const pt = config.pt;\\r\\n    const section = config.section;\\r\\n    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\\r\\n    if (!config) return null;\\r\\n    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\\r\\n    if (selected?.length) {\\r\\n      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\\r\\n      // same run, a different list is a different one.\\r\\n      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const shape = JSON.stringify({\\r\\n      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\\r\\n      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\\r\\n      pts: config.pts ?? [],\\r\\n      sections: config.sections ?? [],\\r\\n      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\\r\\n      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\\r\\n      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\\r\\n      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\\r\\n      count: config.count ?? null,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\\r\\nexport async function loadDrillResumeSession(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  legacyKey?: string | null,\\r\\n): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key) return null;\\r\\n  // Hydration compares the locally saved timestamp with the account row before\\r\\n  // returning. Reading the cloud first could bypass a newer unsynced answer.\\r\\n  let current: DrillResumeSession | null;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\'[drill:section] local resume read failed\', error);\\r\\n    current = null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (accountSectionKey(key)) {\\r\\n    const backup = readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n    if (backup && (!current || backup.updated_at > current.updated_at)) current = backup;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const account = await readSectionFromAccount(userId, key);\\r\\n      if (account && (!current || account.updated_at >= current.updated_at)) {\\r\\n        if (backup) clearSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n        return account;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (current) {\\r\\n        // A backup can be newer than IndexedDB. The runner will sync it as soon\\r\\n        // as it mounts; syncing the stale IndexedDB row here would overwrite it.\\r\\n        if (current !== backup) await syncSectionToAccount(userId, key);\\r\\n        return current;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[drill:section] account read failed, checking local backup\', error);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\\r\\n  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Write the local safety copy and confirm the account write for official sections. */\\r\\nexport async function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key) return;\\r\\n  const backupStamp = accountSectionKey(key) ? writeSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key, snapshot) : null;\\r\\n  await queueAccountWrite(userId, key, async () => {\\r\\n    await assertActiveTestLeg(userId, key);\\r\\n    await savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\\r\\n    if (accountSectionKey(key)) await syncSectionToAccount(userId, key);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (backupStamp && readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key)?.updated_at === backupStamp) {\\r\\n    clearSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function hasPendingSectionDeviceBackup(userId: string, key: string | null): boolean {\\r\\n  return !!userId && !!key && !!readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\\r\\n * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\\r\\n * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function completeDrillResumeSession(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key) return;\\r\\n  await queueAccountWrite(userId, key, async () => {\\r\\n    await assertActiveTestLeg(userId, key);\\r\\n    await completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\\r\\n    if (accountSectionKey(key)) await syncSectionToAccount(userId, key);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (accountSectionKey(key)) clearSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Is this stored row worth resuming?\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\\r\\n * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\\r\\n * resumable when it is still in progress and the student answered something.\\r\\n * Official sections are resumable as soon as they start, including before the\\r\\n * first answer, because their fixed question order is itself worth restoring.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    !!stored &&\\r\\n    stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\\r\\n    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\\r\\n    (stored.attempts.length > 0 || accountSectionKey(stored.pool_slug))\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Was this row\'s section clock already spent when it was stored?\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A row written at or after the buzzer carries elapsed_ms equal to the budget\\r\\n * (the countdown reports zero, so the spend is the whole budget). Restoring it\\r\\n * as an ordinary resume opens the section on 00:00 with no buzzer, because the\\r\\n * regulation mark it also carries tells the buzzer effect it has already\\r\\n * asked. The runner uses this to open such a row on the buzzer instead: the\\r\\n * answers are kept, the clock is not handed back, and the student is asked to\\r\\n * finish and score the section as it was sat.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Untimed runs have no budget and are never \\"spent\\".\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function isSpentDrillSession(\\r\\n  stored: DrillResumeSession | null,\\r\\n  budgetMs: number | undefined,\\r\\n): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!stored || !budgetMs || !Number.isFinite(budgetMs)) return false;\\r\\n  return (stored.elapsed_ms ?? 0) >= budgetMs;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ebc63d","wall_time_seconds":3.5193279,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

});\\r\\n    }, 20_000);\\r\\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\\r\\n  }, [genericResumeKey, user, session, hasTimer, ptRun, pauseForSectionSave]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n Returns null when there is nothing to\\r\\n * resume, when the row is corrupt, or when persistence is unavailable — all of\\r\\n * which mean \\"start fresh\\", never \\"block the student\\".\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function loadPracticeSession(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  poolSlug: string,\\r\\n): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !poolSlug) return null;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\\r\\n    // Wait for cloud hydration first, otherwise a cold load on a second device\\r\\n    // reports \\"nothing to resume\\" while the row is still in flight.\\r\\n    await persist.ready();\\r\\n    const raw = await persist.get<unknown>(TABLES.practice_sessions, practiceSessionIdFor(userId, poolSlug));\\r\\n    if (!raw) return null;\\r\\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw);\\r\\n    if (!parsed.success) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[practice-session] stored row failed validation, ignoring\', {\\r\\n        poolSlug,\\r\\n        issues: parsed.error.issues,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      return null;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const migrated = migratePracticeSession(parsed.data);\\r\\n    if (migrated !== parsed.data) {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, migrated.id, migrated);\\r\\n      } catch (error) {\\r\\n        // A sync failure must not hide an otherwise usable local resume row.\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[practice-session] migration write failed\', { poolSlug, error });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return migrated;\\r\\n  } catch (e) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\'[practice-session] load failed, starting fresh\', { poolSlug, error: e });\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Read the stored session for every practice pool, for the index UI. */\\r\\nexport async function loadAllPracticeSessions(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<Record<string, PracticeSession>> {\\r\\n  const slugs = Object.keys(MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS);\\r\\n"}'}]

Two\\r\\n * things used to be dropped: the answers a student changed their mind about,\\r\\n * and the clock they had already spent. Coming back to a fresh 35:00 on a\\r\\n * section with fifteen minutes left in it makes the score meaningless.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from \'vitest\';\\r\\nimport { __setPersistenceForTests } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\\r\\nimport type { Persistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/Persistence\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  completePracticeSession,\\r\\n  loadPracticeSession,\\r\\n  migratePracticeSession,\\r\\n  PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n  practiceSessionIdFor,\\r\\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n} from \'./practiceSessionStore\';\\r\\nimport { PracticeSession, TABLES } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * In-memory stand-in for the shared bootcamp_kv layer. `readDelays` holds\\r\\n * each successive read for that many ms, the way IndexedDB can, so two\\r\\n * writers can interleave; reads past the list return at once.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst makeStore = (readDelays: number[] = []): Persistence => {\\r\\n  const rows = new Map<string, unknown>();\\r\\n  const delays = [...readDelays];\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    ready: () => Promise.resolve(),\\r\\n    get: <T,>(table: string, id: string) =>\\r\\n      new Promise<T | null>((resolve) =>\\r\\n        setTimeout(() => resolve((rows.get(`${table}/${id}`) as T) ?? null), delays.shift() ?? 0),\\r\\n      ),\\r\\n    set: <T,>(table: string, id: string, record: T) => {\\r\\n      rows.set(`${table}/${id}`, record);\\r\\n      return Promise.resolve();\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    remove: (table: string, id: string) => {\\r\\n      rows.delete(`${table}/${id}`);\\r\\n      return Promise.resolve();\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    list: () => Promise.resolve([]),\\r\\n    putBlob: () => Promise.resolve(),\\r\\n    getBlob: () => Promise.resolve(null),\\r\\n    removeBlob: () => Promise.resolve(),\\r\\n    transaction: (fn) => fn({ get: () => Promise.resolve(null), set: () => Promise.resolve() }),\\r\\n  } as Persistence;\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst USER = \'11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111\';\\r\\nconst POOL = { slug: \'drill::section::pt130-s1\', completionId: \'drill::section::pt130-s1\' };\\r\\n\\r\\ndescribe(\'PT118 legacy resume migration\', () => {\\r\\n  const legacy = PracticeSession.parse({\\r\\n    id: USER,\\r\\n    user_id: USER,\\r\\n    pool_slug: \'PT118-S1-Q26\'],\\r\\n    current_index: 2,\\r\\n    attempts: [\\r\\n      { qid: \'PT118-S1-Q21\', selected_answer: \'E\', correct: false, time_ms: 1234 },\\r\\n      { qid: \'PT118-S1-Q25\', selected_answer: \'E\', correct: false, time_ms: 2345 },\\r\\n      { qid: \'PT118-S1-Q26\', selected_answer: \'A\', correct: false, time_ms: 3456 },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n    regulation_answered_qids: [\'PT118-S1-Q21\', \'PT118-S1-Q25\', \'PT118-S1-Q26\'],\\r\\n    status: \'in_progress\',\\r\\n    started_at: \'2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z\',\\r\\n    updated_at: \'2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z\',\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'preserves question content, current place and first duplicate answer\', () => {\\r\\n    const migrated = migratePracticeSession(legacy);\\r\\n    expect(migrated.question_queue).toEqual([\\r\\n      \'PT118-S1-Q4\', \'PT118-S1-Q6\', \'PT118-S1-Q22\', \'PT118-S1-Q26\',\\r\\n    ]);\\r\\n    expect(migrated.current_index).toBe(2);\\r\\n ## assistant

Implemented LR recovery in six files.

- Official section hydration performs no account writes, including schema migrations.
- Newer or offline local clock/answers remain pending and paused until guarded Retry save.
- Another active device prevents recovery writes and preserves the backup.
- Failed hydration without a local copy hides questions and offers Retry loading drill.
- Timed sections cannot bypass hydration through `initialIndex`.

Verified: 45 focused tests passed, changed-file eslint has no errors. Root must finish browser verification, integrated typecheck/build, and release. An earlier typecheck failed in the concurrent Activity hook, outside these files.

Still open: active-device check and account write are separate operations. Strict concurrent-device protection requires an atomic server contract. No backend/account mutations, commits, or pushes performed.

A newer local\\r\\n *     practice session is queued for sync instead of overwritten.\\r\\n *   - Blobs (audio) stay local only.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport type { Persistence, PersistenceTx } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { V1Persistence } from \'./V1Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from \'./LocalStoragePersistence\';\\r\\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from \'./IndexedDBPersistence\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst FLUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 800;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction updatedAt(value: unknown): number {\\r\\n  if (!value || typeof value !== \'object\') return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n  const timestamp = (value as { updated_at?: unknown }).updated_at;\\r\\n  if (typeof timestamp !== \'string\') return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n  const parsed = Date.parse(timestamp);\\r\\n  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface PendingWrite {\\r\\n  table: string;\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  data: unknown | null; // null = delete\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport class SupabaseSyncedPersistence implements Persistence {\\r\\n  private local: V1Persistence;\\r\\n  private pending = new Map<string, PendingWrite>();\\r\\n  private inFlightWrites = new Map<string, PendingWrite>();\\r\\n  private flushInFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;\\r\\n  private flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\\r\\n  private hydrated = false;\\r\\n  private readyPromise: Promise<void>;\\r\\n\\r\\n  constructor(private userId: string) {\\r\\n    this.local = new V1Persistence(\\r\\n      new LocalStoragePersistence(userId),\\r\\n      new IndexedDBPersistence(userId),\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    // Kick off cloud hydration. The UI does not block on it, but callers that\\r\\n    // must not fabricate a default over real cloud data await ready() first.\\r\\n    // hydrate() never rejects (it catches internally), so ready() always resolves.\\r\\n    this.readyPromise = this.hydrate();\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Best-effort flush on tab close.\\r\\n    if (typeof window !== \'undefined\') {\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'beforeunload\', () => {\\r\\n        void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      window.addEventListener(\'visibilitychange\', () => {\\r\\n        if (document.visibilityState === \'hidden\') void this.flush();\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  ready(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    return this.readyPromise;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private key(table: string, id: string): string {\\r\\n    return `${table}::${id}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async hydrate(): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    if (this.hydrated || !this.userId || this.userId === \'anon\') return;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n        .select(\'table_name, record_id, data\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'user_id\', this.userId);\\r\\n      if (error) {\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate failed\', error.message);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      for (const row of data ?? []) {\\r\\n        try {\\r\\n          const key = this.key(row.table_name, row.record_id);\\r\\n          // A write made while the cloud request was in flight owns this row.\\r\\n          // && updatedAt(local) > updatedAt(row.data)) {\\r\\n            this.pending.set(key, { table: row.table_name, id: row.record_id, data: local });\\r\\n            this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n            continue;\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          await this.local.set(row.table_name, row.record_id, row.data as object);\\r\\n        } catch (e) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate row failed\', e);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      this.hydrated = true;\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] hydrate threw\', e);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  private scheduleFlush(): void {\\r\\n    if (this.flushTimer) clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);\\r\\n    this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {\\r\\n      void this.flush();\\r\\n .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n          .upsert(rows, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n        if (error) {\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] upsert failed\', error.message);\\r\\n          // Re-queue on failure.\\r\\n          for (const b of upserts) {\\r\\n            const key = this.key(b.table, b.id);\\r\\n            if (!this.pending.has(key)) this.pending.set(key, b);\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      for (const d of deletes) {\\r\\n        const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n          .delete()\\r\\n          .eq(\'user_id\', this.userId)\\r\\n          .eq(\'table_name\', d.table)\\r\\n the cloud row is\\r\\n          // intentionally preserved.\\r\\n          console.warn(\'[SupabaseSyncedPersistence] delete denied/failed (cloud row preserved)\', error.message);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n this.key(item.table, item.id);\\r\\n        if (this.inFlightWrites.get(key) === item) this.inFlightWrites.delete(key);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // ── Persistence API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\n  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null> {\\r\\n    return this.local.get<T>(table, id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    await this.local.set(table, id, record);\\r\\n    this.pending.set(this.key(table, id), { table, id, data: record });\\r\\n    this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n    await this.local.remove(table, id);\\r\\n    this.pending.set(this.key(table, id), { table, id, data: null });\\r\\n    this.scheduleFlush();\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]> {\\r\\n    return this.local.list<T>(table, filter);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> { return this.local.putBlob(key, blob); }\\r\\n  getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null> { return this.local.getBlob(key); }\\r\\n  removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void> { return this.local.removeBlob(key); }\\r\\n\\r\\n  async transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\\r\\n    // Wrap the inner tx so writes inside the transaction also get queued for sync.\\r\\n    // The callbacks are arrow functions, so `this` stays lexically bound here.\\r\\n    return this.local.transaction(async (innerTx) => {\\r\\n      const tx: PersistenceTx = {\\r\\n        get: <U>(t: string, i: string) => innerTx.get<U>(t, i),\\r\\n        set: async <U>(t: string, i: string, r: U) => {\\r\\n          await '{"chunk_id":"89783c","wall_time_seconds":1.5352516999999999,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":382,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Persistence factory.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * For signed-in users we use SupabaseSyncedPersistence: local-first reads/writes\\r\\n * with background autosave to the `bootcamp_kv` table so every profile keeps\\r\\n * all of their bootcamp progress in the cloud and across devices.\\r\\n * For anonymous callers we fall back to local-only V1Persistence.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport type { Persistence } from \'./Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { V1Persistence } from \'./V1Persistence\';\\r\\nimport { LocalStoragePersistence } from \'./LocalStoragePersistence\';\\r\\nimport { IndexedDBPersistence } from \'./IndexedDBPersistence\';\\r\\nimport { SupabaseSyncedPersistence } from \'./SupabaseSyncedPersistence\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst _cache: Map<string, Persistence> = new Map();\\r\\nlet _testOverride: Persistence | null = null;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Returns the active adapter scoped to the supplied userId. */\\r\\nexport function getPersistence(userId: string = \'anon\'): Persistence {\\r\\n  if (_testOverride) return _testOverride;\\r\\n  const existing = _cache.get(userId);\\r\\n  if (existing) return existing;\\r\\n  const created: Persistence =\\r\\n    userId && userId !== \'anon\'\\r\\n      ? new SupabaseSyncedPersistence(userId)\\r\\n      : new V1Persistence(\\r\\n          new LocalStoragePersistence(userId),\\r\\n          new IndexedDBPersistence(userId),\\r\\n        );\\r\\n  _cache.set(userId, created);\\r\\n  return created;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Test hook — inject a fake. Passing null clears any override and cache. */\\r\\nexport function __setPersistenceForTests(p: Persistence | null): void {\\r\\n  _testOverride = p;\\r\\n  if (p === null) _cache.clear();\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Fixed the underlying autosync bypass.

Official LR section and PT-leg records now stay out of generic hydration, ordinary-write, and transaction background queues. Newer local answers and clocks remain intact. Only the runner’s guarded, acknowledged save writes them to the account.

Verified 32 persistence tests, including real adapter recovery on another active device with zero upserts after timer expiry and explicit flush. Earlier 19 UI tests passed. Adapter eslint passed.

Eight files changed overall. No commit, push, deployment, or account mutations. Atomic cross-device protection still requires a server contract.
